:MAIN_DeadBust
while not Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
wait 250
    if
        Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    then
        goto @MAIN_Loop
    end
end
    
:MAIN_Loop
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
wait 0
    if
        not Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    then
        goto @MAIN_DeadBust
    else
        goto @Main_Loop
    end
end

:Barrier
script_name 'Barrier'

:Barrier_10
wait 0
if
    Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
then
    if
        $HomeSafe == 1
    then
        if
            $Tool_Mission_Passed == 1
        then
            if
                $Limey_Passed == 1
            then
                if
                    $Boss_Killed == 1
                then
                    goto @Barrier_900
                else
                    goto @Barrier_600
                end
            else
                goto @Barrier_470
            end
        else
            goto @Barrier_300
        end
    else
        goto @Barrier_15
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_1000
end

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 1: Early Game / Lockdown ($HomeSafe == 0) — 0.2 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_15
wait 0
if
    $HomeSafe == 0
then
    World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(0.2)
    World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(0.2)

    if or
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAITI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'GOLFC')
    then
        $PlayerOutside = 1
        goto @Barrier_16
    else
        goto @Barrier_200
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_16
Text.PrintHelpString("Leave this area at once!")
$PlayerHealth = Player.GetHealth($PLAYER1)
if
    $PlayerHealth > 20
then
    $PlayerHealth -= 10
else
    $PlayerHealth -= 1
end
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, $PlayerHealth)
wait 1000
if
    $PlayerHealth < 5
then
    goto @Barrier_500
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_200
wait 0
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
then
    $PlayerOutside = 0
    Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, 100)
    goto @Barrier_10
else
    goto @Barrier_15
end

// STAGE 4: Post-Mission 3 ($Limey_Passed == 1) — 0.8 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_600
wait 3000
if
    $Boss_Killed == 0
then
    World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(2.0)
    World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(2.0)

    if or
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAITI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'GOLFC')
    then
        Weather.Release()
        $PlayerOutside = 0
        goto @Barrier_10
    else
        goto @Barrier_700
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

wait 1000
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
then
    $PlayerOutside = 0
    goto @Barrier_10
else
    goto @Barrier_750
end

:Barrier_750
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
then
    Weather.Force(WeatherType.Foggy)
    Text.PrintHelpString("Leave this area at once!")
    $PlayerOutside = 1
    goto @Barrier_800
else
    goto @Barrier_600
end

:Barrier_800
wait 4000
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
then
    $PlayerHealth = Player.GetHealth($PLAYER1)
    if
        $PlayerHealth > 20
    then
        $PlayerHealth -= 10
    else
        $PlayerHealth -= 1
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_600
end
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, $PlayerHealth)
wait 1000
if
    $PlayerHealth < 5
then
    goto @Barrier_500
else
    goto @Barrier_700
end

:PackageToggle
script_name 'PakTog'
$FirstPackageTold = 0                   // Flag to ensure tutorial only triggers once

while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    $Packages = Game.GetCollectablesCollected()

    // Check if player just collected their first package to trigger full tutorial
    if and
        $Packages >= 1
        $FirstPackageTold == 0
    then
        $FirstPackageTold = 1

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHL1')
        wait 5000

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHL2')
        wait 5000

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHLP')
    end

    // Check if the package hunt is still active (not at max 83)
    if
        not $Packages == 83
    then
        // Press 'O' to turn OFF radar tracker
        if
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.O)
        then
            Text.PrintHelp('PACKOFF')
            $PackHunt = 0
            Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
            terminate_all_scripts_with_this_name 'PACKAGE'
            wait 500
        else

        // Press 'P' to turn ON radar tracker
            if Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.P)
            then
                $PackHunt = 1
                Text.PrintHelp('PACKON')
                wait 0
                start_new_script @PACKAGE
                wait 500
            end
        end
    else
        // Player pressed toggle after collecting everything
        if or
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.O)
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.P)
        then
            Text.PrintHelp('PACKDON')
        end
    end
end


// ============================================================================
// RADAR GUIDE & PACKAGE REWARDS THREAD
// ============================================================================
:PACKAGE
script_name 'PACKAGE'

0@ = 0                                  // Active Target Package Index (0 to 82)
1@ = 1                                  // Blip Redraw Flag (1 = Needs New Blip)

while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 250

    // STRICT GUARD: Only run if radar tracking is toggled ON
    if $PackHunt == 1
    then
        $Packages = Game.GetCollectablesCollected()

        // --- MILESTONE REWARDS LOGIC ---
        if $Packages >= 10
        then
            if 5@ == 0
            then
                5@ = 1
                Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
                Text.PrintHelp('PICK1')
                $StarfishArmor = Pickup.Create(#BODYARMOUR, PickupType.OnStreet, -536.524, -304.216, 11.4)
                $StarfishBlade = Pickup.Create(#KNIFECUR, PickupType.OnStreet, -499.192, -245.979, 10.4)
            end
        end

        if $Packages >= 20
        then
            if 6@ == 0
            then
                6@ = 1
                Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
                Text.PrintHelp('PICK2')
                $StarfishPython = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#PYTHON, PickupType.OnStreet, 300, -508.021, -245.979, 10.4)
            end
        end

        if $Packages >= 45
        then
            if 7@ == 0
            then
                7@ = 1
                Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
                Text.PrintHelp('PICK3')
                $StarfishSniper = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#SNIPER, PickupType.OnStreet, 40, -510.175, -245.979, 10.4)
            end
        end

        if $Packages >= 60
        then
            if 8@ == 0
            then
                8@ = 1
                Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
                Text.PrintHelp('PICK4')
                $StarfishMinigun = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#MINIGUN, PickupType.OnStreet, 5000, -515.431, -245.979, 10.4)
                CarGenerator.Switch($Squalo, 101)
            end
        end

        if $Packages >= 80
        then
            if 9@ == 0
            then
                9@ = 1
                Text.PrintHelpString("Nice! That's all the packages! Here's a bonus!")
                Player.AddScore($PLAYER1, 50000)
                play_audio_stream_1channel "Audio/SSA/miscom.wav", false, 0.5
                Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            end
        end

        // --- RADAR TRACKING LOGIC ---
        if 0@ <= 82
        then
            gosub @Get_Package_Coords

            // Draw/Redraw blip on active target package
            if 1@ == 1
            then
                1@ = 0
                Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
                $CurrentPackBlip = Blip.AddForCoord($PackCoordX, $PackCoordY, $PackCoordZ)
                Blip.ChangeColor($CurrentPackBlip, BlipColor.Red)
            end

            // PROXIMITY CHECK: Only advance 0@ IF player actually collects THIS target package
            if Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, $PackCoordX, $PackCoordY, $PackCoordZ, 3.5, 3.5, 3.5, False)
            then
                0@ += 1                  // Target collected! Advance to next coordinate index
                1@ = 1                  // Signal blip redraw for the next package
                wait 1000                // Debounce pause so index doesn't double-increment
            end
        else
            // All 83 package spots cleared
            Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
            terminate_this_script
        end
    end
end

:Help
script_name 'Help'

:Help_10
wait 250
if
    Pickup.HasBeenCollected($PhoneHelp)
then
    goto @Help_71
else
    if
        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($PhoneHelp)
    then
        goto @Help_400
    else
        if
            Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BUSTED_HELP1)
        then
            goto @Help_400
        else
            if
                Pickup.HasBeenCollected($WASTED_HELP1)
            then
                goto @Help_682
            else
                if
                    Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachJettyInfo)
                then
                    goto @Help_777
                else
                    if or
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachBribe1)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($HavanaBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachBribe2)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($StarfishBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($ViceportBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($VicePointBribe)
                    then
                        goto @Help_3184
                    else
                        if
                            Pickup.HasBeenCollected($MarinaHelp)
                        then
                            goto @Help_3698
                        else
                            if
                                Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BarrierHelp)
                            then
                                goto @Help_4210
                            else
                                if
                                    $Barrier1 == 1
                                then
                                    goto @Help_5210
                                else
                                    if
                                        $Barrier2 == 1
                                    then
                                        goto @Help_5420
                                    else
                                        if
                                            Player.IsOnAnyBike($PLAYER1)
                                        then
                                            goto @Help_5600
                                        else
                                            if
                                                $PRINT_CAR_HELP == 0
                                            then
                                                goto @Help_5800
                                            else
                                                if and
                                                    $Barrier1Done == 1
                                                    $Barrier2Done == 1
                                                then
                                                    $HelpDone == 1
                                                    goto @Help_6000
                                                else
                                                    goto @Help_10
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

:JewelryRobbery
script_name 'Jewelry'

:Jewelry_Init
wait 0
Streaming.LoadSpecialCharacter(16, 'S_KEEP')
Streaming.RequestModel(#SPECIAL16)

:Jewelry_LoadModel
wait 0
if
    Streaming.HasSpecialCharacterLoaded(16)
then
    $JewelryClerk = Char.Create(PedType.CivMale, #SPECIAL16, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862)
    Char.SetHeading($JewelryClerk, 268.67)
    Char.SetHealth($JewelryClerk, 100)
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
    goto @Jewelry_MainLoop
else
    goto @Jewelry_LoadModel
end

:Jewelry_MainLoop
wait 0

if
    Char.IsDead($JewelryClerk)
then
    goto @Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
end

if
    Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, 15.0, 15.0, 5.0, False)
then
    if
        Player.IsTargetingChar($PLAYER1, $JewelryClerk)
    then
        goto @Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
    else
        $AimStartTime = 0
        $RobberyMoney = 0
    end
else
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
end

goto @Jewelry_MainLoop

:Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
wait 0
// Capture starting game time on first frame of aiming
if
    $AimStartTime == 0
then
    Clock.GetGameTimer($AimStartTime)
    Char.SetCrouch($JewelryClerk, True, 9999999)
end

Clock.GetGameTimer($CurrentTime)
set_var_int_to_var_int $ElapsedTime = $CurrentTime
sub_int_var_from_int_var $ElapsedTime -= $AimStartTime

// Award compounding score every 1000ms (1 second) of aiming
if
    $ElapsedTime >= 1000
then
    $RobberyMoney += 50
    Player.AddScore($PLAYER1, 50)
    Text.PrintBigString("+$50", 1000, TextStyle.MiddleSmaller)
    Sound.AddOneOffSound(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, ScriptSound.SoundPartMissionComplete)
    // Reset timer anchor for the next interval
    Clock.GetGameTimer($AimStartTime)
end

// Trigger alarm if player holds aim for 10+ seconds OR stops aiming after starting
if or
    $RobberyMoney >= 500
    not Player.IsTargetingChar($PLAYER1, $JewelryClerk)
then
    goto @Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
else
    goto @Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
end

:Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
Text.PrintHelpString("He called the cops! Lose the heat!")
Player.AlterWantedLevel($PLAYER1, 2)

if
    $AlarmOn == 0
then
    $JewelryAlarm = Sound.AddContinuous(379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, ScriptSound.SoundBankAlarmLoop)
    $AlarmOn = 1
end

if
    not Char.IsDead($JewelryClerk)
then
    Char.SetCrouch($JewelryClerk, True, 9999999)
end

:Jewelry_AlarmLoop
wait 0
if or
    not Player.IsWantedLevelGreater($PLAYER1, 0)
    Hud.AreWantedStarsFlashing()
then
    Sound.Remove($JewelryAlarm)
    $AlarmOn = 0
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
    goto @Jewelry_ResetWait
else
    goto @Jewelry_AlarmLoop
end

:Jewelry_ResetWait
while Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, 30.0, 30.0, 10.0, False)
    wait 1000
    if
        not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, 30.0, 30.0, 10.0, False)
    then
        Char.Delete($JewelryClerk)
        $JewelryClerk = Char.Create(PedType.CivMale, #SPECIAL16, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862)
        Char.SetHeading($JewelryClerk, 268.67)
        Char.SetHealth($JewelryClerk, 100)
        goto @Jewelry_MainLoop
    end
end

:VehHeal
script_name 'VehHeal'

:VehHeal_101
while not Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    if
        Player.CanStartMission($PLAYER1)
    then
        if
            Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
        then
            $MyCar = Player.StoreCarIsInNoSave($PLAYER1)
            goto @VehHeal_102
        end
    end
end

:VehHeal_102
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    Car.GetHealth($MyCar, $CarHealth)
    $CarHealthBar = $CarHealth
    $CarHealthBar *= 100
    $CarHealthBar /= 1000
    Hud.DisplayCounterWithString($CarHealthBar, CounterDisplay.Bar, 'VEHHEAL')

    if
        not Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    then
        Car.Delete($MyCar)
        Hud.ClearCounter($CarHealthBar)
        start_new_script @VehHeal
        terminate_this_script
    end

    if
        not Player.IsInCar($PLAYER1, $MyCar)
    then
        Hud.ClearCounter($CarHealthBar)
        goto @VehHeal_101
    end
end

        $ANY_HEADING <= 90.0

    else
        goto @Pizza_10

else
    goto @Pizza_10

            not Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
        then

            wait 500
            Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
            Player.SetCoordinates($PLAYER1, 315.7423, -1164.2609, 11.0712)
            wait 500
            Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.In)
            wait 500

        end

        not Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.N)
    then
        goto @Water_70
    else
        goto @Water_20
    end
end

:Endgame
script_name 'Endgame'

:Endgame_LoadModels
wait 250
Streaming.RequestModel(#SABRE)
Streaming.RequestModel(#WFYG1)

:Endgame_WaitModels
wait 0
if and
    Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#SABRE)
    Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#WFYG1)
then
    Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
    $Sabre = Car.Create(#SABRE, -535.8, -323.328, 13.62)
    wait 250
    $EndOfGamePed = Char.Create(PedType.CivFemale, #WFYG1, -530.692, -315.928, 13.62)
    wait 250
else
    goto @Endgame_WaitModels
end

Camera.PointAtChar($EndOfGamePed, CameraMode.FollowPed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500

:Endgame_Enter
Char.SetObjEnterCarAsDriver($scplayer, $Sabre)
Char.SetObjEnterCarAsPassenger($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)

:Endgame_CheckBoarding
wait 500
if or
    Char.IsInCar($scplayer, $Sabre)
    Char.IsInCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
then
    goto @Endgame_Go
end

// Check if they got stuck while trying to enter
if or
    Char.IsStuck($EndOfGamePed)
    Char.IsStuck($scplayer)
then
    // Reset positions to try entering again without re-spawning vehicles
    Char.SetCoordinates($EndOfGamePed, -530.692, -315.928, 13.62)
    Char.SetCoordinates($scplayer, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56)
    wait 500
    goto @Endgame_Enter
end

goto @Endgame_CheckBoarding

:Endgame_Go
Camera.PointAtCar($Sabre, CameraMode.BehindCar, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Car.AddUpsidedownCheck($Sabre)
Car.GotoCoordinatesAccurate($Sabre, 240.4, -1280.2, 10.0)
Audio.PlayEndOfGameTune()

:Endgame_Stop
while not Car.IsStoppedInArea2D($Sabre, 235.4, -1275.2, 245.6, -1285.6, False)
    wait 0
    if
        Car.Locate2D($Sabre, 235.0, -1275.0, 10.0, 10.0, False)
    then
        Car.SetMission($Sabre, CarMission.None)
        goto @Endgame_Finish
    end
    if or
        Car.IsInWater($Sabre)
        Car.IsUpsidedown($Sabre)
    then
        goto @Endgame_ResetCutscene
    end
end

:Endgame_ResetCutscene
$ONMISSION = 0
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500
if
    Char.DoesExist($EndOfGamePed)
then
    Char.Delete($EndOfGamePed)
end
if
    not Car.IsDead($Sabre)
then
    Car.Delete($Sabre)
end
Char.SetCoordinates($scplayer, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56)
goto @Endgame_LoadModels

:Endgame_Finish
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500
Camera.PointAtChar($EndOfGamePed, CameraMode.FollowPed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Audio.StopEndOfGameTune()
Char.SetObjLeaveCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
Char.SetObjLeaveCar($scplayer, $Sabre)
Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(20)
$Endgame = 1
$ONMISSION = 0

wait 1000
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500
Char.SetObjDestroyCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
Text.Print('Endgame', 5000, 0) // Go watch the mindless destruction!
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#SABRE)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYG1)
terminate_this_script
